FILE: vigil-frontend/src/components/PatientHeader.tsx
================================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Activity, CheckSquare, Droplet, Heart, SlidersHorizontal, Wind } from 'lucide-react';
import { Patient, ScreenId } from '../types';
import { PATIENTS } from '../data/mockData';
import { useTelemetry } from '../hooks/useTelemetry';
import { BackendPatientDetail, BackendPatientSummary, fetchPatientRisk, fetchPatients } from '../services/api';
import { getRiskBand, RISK_BANDS } from '../constants/clinicalDisplay';

interface PatientHeaderProps {
  patient: Patient;
  onSelectPatient: (patient: Patient) => void;
  currentScreen: ScreenId;
  onNavigate: (screen: ScreenId) => void;
}

export const PatientHeader: React.FC<PatientHeaderProps> = ({ patient, onSelectPatient, currentScreen, onNavigate }) => {
  const latestVital = patient.vitals[patient.vitals.length - 1];
  const { telemetry } = useTelemetry(patient.bed);
  const [backendData, setBackendData] = useState<BackendPatientDetail | null>(null);
  const [apiPatients, setApiPatients] = useState<BackendPatientSummary[]>([]);
  const [backendUnavailable, setBackendUnavailable] = useState(false);
  const [isPatientLoading, setIsPatientLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    setBackendUnavailable(false);
    setIsPatientLoading(true);
    setBackendData(null);
    fetchPatientRisk(patient.bed, false)
      .then((detail) => { if (!cancelled) setBackendData(detail); })
      .catch(() => { if (!cancelled) setBackendUnavailable(true); })
      .finally(() => { if (!cancelled) setIsPatientLoading(false); });
    fetchPatients()
      .then((patients) => { if (!cancelled) setApiPatients(patients); })
      .catch(() => { if (!cancelled) setApiPatients([]); });
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [patient.bed]);

  const displayHr = telemetry?.hr ?? backendData?.vitals.HR ?? (backendUnavailable ? latestVital.hr : '—');
  const displayMap = telemetry?.map ?? backendData?.vitals.MAP ?? (backendUnavailable ? latestVital.map : '—');
  const displaySpo2 = telemetry?.o2sat ?? backendData?.vitals.O2Sat ?? (backendUnavailable ? latestVital.spo2 : '—');
  const displayResp = telemetry?.resp ?? backendData?.vitals.Resp ?? (backendUnavailable ? latestVital.rr : '—');
  const displayedRisk = backendData?.sepsis_risk_percentage;
  const lactate = backendData?.vitals.Lactate ?? (backendUnavailable ? patient.lactate : '—');
  const selectorItems = apiPatients.length ? apiPatients : PATIENTS.map((item) => ({ id: item.id, bed: item.bed, name: item.name, primary_dx: item.primaryDiagnosis }));

  const selectPatient = (bed: string) => {
    const summary = apiPatients.find((item) => item.bed === bed);
    const fallback = PATIENTS.find((item) => item.bed === bed);
    if (summary) onSelectPatient({ ...(fallback ?? patient), id: summary.id, name: summary.name, bed: summary.bed, primaryDiagnosis: summary.primary_dx });
    else if (fallback) onSelectPatient(fallback);
  };

  return (
    <section id="patient-header" className="sticky top-[76px] z-30 border-b border-slate-200/80 bg-white/95 shadow-sm backdrop-blur-xl">
      <div className="page-shell py-2">
        {backendUnavailable && <div role="status" className="mb-2 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-amber-900">Backend unavailable · sample details shown; model risk is unavailable.</div>}
        <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
          <div className="flex min-w-[190px] max-w-[250px] shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h1 className="truncate font-display text-sm font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900">{backendData?.name ?? (backendUnavailable ? patient.name : isPatientLoading ? 'Loading patient…' : patient.bed)}</h1>
                <span className="shrink-0 rounded-md border border-slate-200 bg-white px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-700">{patient.bed}</span>
              </div>
              <label htmlFor="patient-selector-dropdown" className="sr-only">Select demo patient</label>
              <select id="patient-selector-dropdown" value={patient.bed} onChange={(event) => selectPatient(event.target.value)} className="mt-1 w-full truncate rounded-md border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-600 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500">
                {selectorItems.map((item) => <option key={item.id} value={item.bed}>{item.name} · {item.bed}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>
          {(() => {
            const band = displayedRisk === undefined ? null : RISK_BANDS[getRiskBand(displayedRisk)];
            return <div className={`flex h-10 shrink-0 flex-col justify-center rounded-lg border px-2.5 ${band ? `${band.background} ${band.border}` : 'border-slate-200 bg-slate-50'}`}><span className="text-[8px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Estimate{band ? ` · ${getRiskBand(displayedRisk as number)}` : ''}</span><span className={`font-mono text-sm font-bold leading-4 ${band?.text ?? 'text-slate-500'}`}>{displayedRisk === undefined ? (backendUnavailable ? 'Sample' : '—') : `${displayedRisk.toFixed(1)}%`}</span></div>;
          })()}
          <VitalTile icon={<Activity />} label="MAP" value={displayMap} suffix="mmHg" />
          <VitalTile icon={<Heart />} label="HR" value={displayHr} suffix="bpm" />
          <VitalTile icon={<Wind />} label="Respiratory rate" value={displayResp} suffix="/min" />
          <VitalTile icon={<Activity />} label="SpO₂" value={displaySpo2} suffix="%" />
          <VitalTile icon={<Droplet />} label="Lactate" value={lactate} suffix="mmol/L" />
        </div>
        <nav className="flex items-center gap-1 overflow-x-auto border-t border-slate-100 pt-1" aria-label="Patient workflow">
          <WorkflowTab active={currentScreen === 'risk'} onClick={() => onNavigate('risk')} icon={<Activity />} label="Explain" />
          <WorkflowTab active={currentScreen === 'counterfactual'} onClick={() => onNavigate('counterfactual')} icon={<SlidersHorizontal />} label="What-if" />
          <WorkflowTab active={currentScreen === 'orders'} onClick={() => onNavigate('orders')} icon={<CheckSquare />} label="Sandbox" />
        </nav>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

const VitalTile = ({ icon, label, value, suffix }: { icon: React.ReactNode; label: string; value: number | string; suffix: string }) => (
  <div className="flex h-10 shrink-0 flex-col justify-center rounded-lg border border-slate-200/80 bg-slate-50/80 px-2.5">
    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[8px] font-semibold text-slate-500">{React.cloneElement(icon as React.ReactElement<{ className?: string }>, { className: 'h-2.5 w-2.5 text-slate-500' })}{label}</div>
    <div className="whitespace-nowrap font-mono text-xs font-bold leading-4 text-slate-800">{value}<span className="ml-1 text-[8px] font-medium text-slate-400">{suffix}</span></div>
  </div>
);

const WorkflowTab = ({ active, onClick, icon, label }: { active: boolean; onClick: () => void; icon: React.ReactNode; label: string }) => (
  <button onClick={onClick} className={`inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold ${active ? 'bg-[#e9f5f2] text-[#075c57]' : 'text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-slate-800'}`}>
    {React.cloneElement(icon as React.ReactElement<{ className?: string }>, { className: 'h-3 w-3' })}{label}
  </button>
);

================================================================================
FILE: vigil-frontend/src/components/screens/CounterfactualScreen.tsx
================================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Activity, ArrowRight, RotateCcw, Sliders } from 'lucide-react';
import { ScreenId, Patient } from '../../types';
import { BackendPatientDetail, SimulationResult, fetchPatientRisk, simulateCounterfactual } from '../../services/api';
import { displayFeatureName, FEATURE_UNITS } from '../../constants/clinicalDisplay';

interface CounterfactualScreenProps { patient: Patient; onNavigate: (screen: ScreenId) => void; }

const FEATURE_LIMITS: Record<string, { min: number; max: number; step: number; unit: string }> = {
  Lactate: { min: 0.5, max: 8, step: 0.1, unit: 'mmol/L' },
  MAP: { min: 50, max: 110, step: 1, unit: 'mmHg' },
  HR: { min: 40, max: 150, step: 1, unit: 'bpm' },
  Resp: { min: 8, max: 40, step: 1, unit: '/min' },
  Temp: { min: 35, max: 41, step: 0.1, unit: '°C' },
  O2Sat: { min: 50, max: 100, step: 1, unit: '%' },
  WBC: { min: 1, max: 30, step: 0.1, unit: '×10⁹/L' },
  Platelets: { min: 20, max: 500, step: 1, unit: '×10⁹/L' },
  Creatinine: { min: 0.3, max: 8, step: 0.1, unit: 'mg/dL' },
  HCO3: { min: 8, max: 36, step: 0.1, unit: 'mmol/L' },
  BUN: { min: 5, max: 80, step: 1, unit: 'mg/dL' },
  Glucose: { min: 50, max: 300, step: 1, unit: 'mg/dL' },
  Bilirubin_total: { min: 0.1, max: 8, step: 0.1, unit: 'mg/dL' },
};

export const CounterfactualScreen: React.FC<CounterfactualScreenProps> = ({ patient, onNavigate }) => {
  const [patientData, setPatientData] = useState<BackendPatientDetail | null>(null);
  const [overrides, setOverrides] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
  const [result, setResult] = useState<SimulationResult | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isSimulating, setIsSimulating] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    setPatientData(null);
    setResult(null);
    setOverrides({});
    fetchPatientRisk(patient.bed, false)
      .then((data) => { if (!cancelled) setPatientData(data); })
      .catch((reason: unknown) => { if (!cancelled) setError(reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : 'Could not load patient features.'); })
      .finally(() => { if (!cancelled) setIsLoading(false); });
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [patient.bed]);

  const activeOverrides = useMemo(() => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(overrides).filter(([feature, value]) => patientData?.vitals[feature] !== value)), [overrides, patientData]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!patientData || Object.keys(activeOverrides).length === 0) {
      setResult(null);
      setIsSimulating(false);
      return;
    }
    let cancelled = false;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      setIsSimulating(true);
      setError(null);
      simulateCounterfactual(patient.bed, activeOverrides)
        .then((simulation) => { if (!cancelled) setResult(simulation); })
        .catch((reason: unknown) => { if (!cancelled) setError(reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : 'Simulation request failed.'); })
        .finally(() => { if (!cancelled) setIsSimulating(false); });
    }, 300);
    return () => { cancelled = true; clearTimeout(timer); };
  }, [patient.bed, patientData, activeOverrides]);

  const updateFeature = (feature: string, value: number) => {
    setResult(null);
    setError(null);
    setOverrides((current) => ({ ...current, [feature]: value }));
  };
  const reset = () => { setOverrides({}); setResult(null); setError(null); };

  const applyPreset = (preset: Record<string, number>) => {
    setOverrides(preset);
    setResult(null);
    setError(null);
  };

  return <div className="min-h-screen pb-16"><div className="page-shell pt-6 space-y-6">
    <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-end justify-between gap-4 pb-5 border-b border-slate-200"><div><div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono text-teal-700 font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em]"><Sliders className="w-4 h-4" /> Model feature scenario</div><h1 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight mt-2 font-display">Explore a what-if scenario</h1><p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-2 max-w-2xl">Change a feature value and see how the backend model estimate responds.</p></div><button onClick={reset} disabled={!patientData} className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-700 font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-slate-50"><RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Reset values</button></div>
    <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-xs leading-relaxed text-amber-950"><strong className="mr-1">Scenario only.</strong>Illustrative slider ranges. This changes model inputs; it does not estimate treatment effects or predict clinical outcomes. Interpret results with clinical evaluation.</div>
    {isLoading && <div className="p-8 text-center bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl text-slate-600">Loading patient model inputs…</div>}
    {error && <div role="alert" className="p-4 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-rose-900">{error}</div>}
    {patientData && <>
      <section className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <RiskCard label="Baseline risk" value={`${patientData.sepsis_risk_percentage.toFixed(1)}%`} />
        <RiskCard label="Scenario risk" value={result ? `${result.simulated_risk_percentage.toFixed(1)}%` : '—'} detail={isSimulating ? 'Updating from backend…' : result ? `${result.risk_change_percentage_points > 0 ? '+' : ''}${result.risk_change_percentage_points.toFixed(1)} percentage points` : 'Change a feature to simulate'} />
        <RiskCard label="Features overridden" value={`${Object.keys(activeOverrides).length}`} detail="Values sent to backend model" />
      </section>
      <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-[22px] p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-3 border-b border-slate-100"><div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Activity className="w-4 h-4 text-teal-700" /><h2 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wider font-mono">Model feature inputs</h2></div><span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 text-slate-600">Illustrative ranges</span></div>
        <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-slate-600">Each slider value is sent to the backend model as a new input. The bounds are illustrative reference ranges, not model-derived limits.</p>
        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2" aria-label="Scenario presets">
          <button type="button" onClick={() => applyPreset({ Lactate: Math.max(0.5, Math.min(8, 1.2)) })} disabled={patientData.vitals.Lactate === undefined} className="secondary-action px-3 py-2 text-[11px] disabled:opacity-50">Normalize lactate · 1.2</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => applyPreset({ MAP: 75 })} disabled={patientData.vitals.MAP === undefined} className="secondary-action px-3 py-2 text-[11px] disabled:opacity-50">Raise MAP · 75</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => applyPreset({ Lactate: 1.2, MAP: 75 })} disabled={patientData.vitals.Lactate === undefined || patientData.vitals.MAP === undefined} className="secondary-action px-3 py-2 text-[11px] disabled:opacity-50">Set both values</button>
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-4">{Object.entries(FEATURE_LIMITS).map(([feature, limits]) => {
          const baseline = patientData.vitals[feature];
          if (baseline === undefined) return null;
          const value = overrides[feature] ?? baseline;
          const sliderValue = Math.max(limits.min, Math.min(limits.max, value));
          return <div key={feature} className="p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50/80 border border-slate-200"><div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-3"><label htmlFor={`feature-${feature}`} className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">{displayFeatureName(feature)}</label><span className="font-mono text-sm font-bold text-teal-800">{value} <span className="text-[10px] font-normal text-slate-500">{FEATURE_UNITS[feature] ?? limits.unit}</span></span></div><input id={`feature-${feature}`} aria-label={`${displayFeatureName(feature)} model input`} type="range" min={limits.min} max={limits.max} step={limits.step} value={sliderValue} onChange={(event) => updateFeature(feature, Number(event.target.value))} className="w-full mt-4 accent-teal-700"/><div className="flex justify-between text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500"><span>Patient value: {baseline}</span><span>{limits.min}–{limits.max}</span></div></div>;
        })}</div>
        {result && <div className="mt-5 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200"><h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 mb-3">Server-returned SHAP contributions (log-odds units)</h3><div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-2">{[...result.simulated_contributions].sort((a, b) => Math.abs(b.shap_impact) - Math.abs(a.shap_impact)).map((item) => <div key={item.feature} className="flex justify-between p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-xs"><span>{displayFeatureName(item.feature)}: {item.value} {FEATURE_UNITS[item.feature]}</span><strong className={item.direction === 'increases_risk' ? 'text-rose-700' : 'text-emerald-700'}>{item.shap_impact > 0 ? '+' : ''}{item.shap_impact.toFixed(4)}</strong></div>)}</div></div>}
      </section>
      <div className="surface-muted flex flex-col gap-3 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:px-5">
        <div><p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">Next step</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-600">Continue to the simulated sandbox to review and record your acknowledgement.</p></div>
        <button onClick={() => onNavigate('orders')} className="primary-action min-h-10 px-4 py-2 text-xs">Review sandbox receipt <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /></button>
      </div>
    </>}
  </div></div>;
};

const RiskCard = ({ label, value, detail }: { label: string; value: string; detail?: string }) => <div className="p-5 sm:p-6 rounded-[22px] bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-sm"><div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.12em]">{label}</div><div className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold font-display text-slate-900 mt-3 tracking-tight">{value}</div>{detail && <div className="text-xs text-slate-600 mt-2">{detail}</div>}</div>;

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import { ScreenId } from '../../types';
import { fetchPatients } from '../../services/api';

interface LandingScreenProps { onNavigate: (screen: ScreenId) => void; }

const DEMO_STEPS: { number: string; title: string; detail: string; screen: ScreenId; icon: React.ReactNode }[] = [
  { number: '01', title: 'Select a patient', detail: 'Review the fictional ICU cohort and backend model estimates.', screen: 'queue', icon: <Database /> },
  { number: '02', title: 'Understand the estimate', detail: 'Inspect patient inputs, feature attribution, and narration.', screen: 'risk', icon: <Cpu /> },
  { number: '03', title: 'Explore a scenario', detail: 'Change model features and request a new server-side estimate.', screen: 'counterfactual', icon: <SlidersHorizontal /> },
  { number: '04', title: 'Review the receipt', detail: 'Record a simulated sandbox bundle and inspect its audit hash.', screen: 'orders', icon: <CheckCircle2 /> },
];

export const LandingScreen: React.FC<LandingScreenProps> = ({ onNavigate }) => {
  const [connection, setConnection] = useState<'checking' | 'online' | 'offline'>('checking');
  const [patientCount, setPatientCount] = useState<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetchPatients()
      .then((patients) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setPatientCount(patients.length);
        setConnection('online');

        if (cancelled) return;
        setPatientCount(null);
        setConnection('offline');
      });
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, []);

    <div className="min-h-screen pb-16">
      <section className="px-4 pb-12 pt-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 lg:pb-16 lg:pt-12">

                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-white/15 bg-white/10 px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-white/85">
                    <ShieldCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-teal-300" /> Explainable ICU prototype
                  </span>
                  <span role="status" className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold ${connection === 'online' ? 'bg-emerald-400/15 text-emerald-200' : connection === 'offline' ? 'bg-amber-300/15 text-amber-200' : 'bg-white/10 text-white/70'}`}>
                    {connection === 'online' ? <Wifi className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> : connection === 'offline' ? <WifiOff className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> : <span className="h-3 w-3 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-white/50 border-t-transparent" />}
                    {connection === 'online' ? `API connected · ${patientCount} demo patients` : connection === 'offline' ? 'API offline · sample mode' : 'Connecting to local API'}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <p className="eyebrow !text-teal-300">Vigil AI · Clinical decision support</p>
                <h1 className="mt-4 max-w-2xl font-display text-4xl font-semibold leading-[1.05] tracking-[-0.05em] text-white sm:text-5xl lg:text-[3.7rem]">
                  See what shapes each model estimate.
                </h1>
                <p className="mt-5 max-w-xl text-sm leading-7 text-slate-300 sm:text-base">
                  Inspect patient inputs, understand the strongest contributing factors, and explore how illustrative scenarios change the model output.
                </p>
                <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap gap-3">

                    Start patient review <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />

                <div className="mt-7 flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                  <CircleHelp className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                  Research prototype only. Not clinically validated or intended to guide care.

                </div>
                <div className="mt-2">
                  {DEMO_STEPS.map((step, index) => (
                    <button key={step.number} onClick={() => onNavigate(step.screen)} className="group flex w-full items-start gap-4 border-b border-white/[0.08] py-4 text-left last:border-0">

FILE: vigil-frontend/src/components/screens/OrdersAuditScreen.tsx
================================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Check, CheckSquare, Clipboard, Copy, FileCheck2, Info, Square } from 'lucide-react';
import { ScreenId, Patient, ClinicalOrder } from '../../types';
import { DEFAULT_ORDERS } from '../../data/mockData';
import { BackendPatientDetail, fetchPatientRisk, OrderSignResponse, signOrderBundle } from '../../services/api';

interface OrdersAuditScreenProps {
  patient: Patient;
  onNavigate: (screen: ScreenId) => void;
}

export const OrdersAuditScreen: React.FC<OrdersAuditScreenProps> = ({ patient, onNavigate }) => {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<ClinicalOrder[]>(() => DEFAULT_ORDERS.map((item, index) => ({ ...item, selected: index < 2 })));
  const [reviewerNote, setReviewerNote] = useState('');
  const [patientData, setPatientData] = useState<BackendPatientDetail | null>(null);
  const [isLoadingPatient, setIsLoadingPatient] = useState(true);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [receipt, setReceipt] = useState<OrderSignResponse | null>(null);
  const [receiptEstimate, setReceiptEstimate] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    setPatientData(null);
    setReceipt(null);
    setReceiptEstimate(null);
    setReviewerNote('');
    setItems(DEFAULT_ORDERS.map((item, index) => ({ ...item, selected: index < 2 })));
    setIsLoadingPatient(true);
    fetchPatientRisk(patient.bed, false)
      .then((detail) => { if (!cancelled) setPatientData(detail); })
      .catch(() => { if (!cancelled) setPatientData(null); })
      .finally(() => { if (!cancelled) setIsLoadingPatient(false); });
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [patient.bed]);

  const selectedCount = items.filter((item) => item.selected).length;
  const modelEstimate = patientData?.sepsis_risk_percentage ?? null;
  const payload = useMemo(() => ({
    patient_id: patient.bed,
    clinician_name: 'Demo Reviewer',
    selected_orders: items.filter((item) => item.selected).map((item) => (
      reviewerNote.trim() ? `${item.title} — Reviewer note: ${reviewerNote.trim()}` : item.title
    )),
  }), [items, patient.bed, reviewerNote]);

  const updateSelected = (id: string) => {
    setReceipt(null);
    setError(null);
    setItems((current) => current.map((item) => item.id === id ? { ...item, selected: !item.selected } : item));
  };

  const selectRecommended = () => {
    setReceipt(null);
    setError(null);
    setItems((current) => current.map((item, index) => ({ ...item, selected: index < 2 })));
  };

  const submitReceipt = async () => {
    setIsSubmitting(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const result = await signOrderBundle(payload);
      setReceipt(result);
      setReceiptEstimate(modelEstimate);
      setCopied(false);
    } catch (reason) {
      setError(reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : 'Could not save the simulated review acknowledgement receipt.');
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const copyHash = async () => {
    if (!receipt) return;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(receipt.integrity_signature_sha256);
      setCopied(true);
      window.setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1800);
    } catch {
      setError('Could not copy the receipt hash. Select and copy it manually.');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen pb-16">
      <div className="page-shell space-y-6 pt-6">

        <header className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 pb-5 md:flex-row md:items-end md:justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="eyebrow flex items-center gap-2"><FileCheck2 className="h-4 w-4" /> Reviewer workflow</p>
            <h1 className="page-title mt-2 scroll-mt-44">Review acknowledgement receipt</h1>
            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">Record which parts of the decision-support demo you reviewed.</p>
          </div>
          <span className="w-fit rounded-full border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-amber-800">SIMULATED SANDBOX</span>
        </header>

        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-4 text-xs leading-relaxed text-amber-950">
          <Info className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-amber-700" />
          <p><strong>Simulated sandbox — not a real EHR, not a digital signature.</strong> This demo records reviewer acknowledgement strings only; it does not create clinical orders. No guideline linkage is provided.</p>
        </div>

        <div className="grid items-start gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1.1fr_0.9fr]">
          <section className="surface p-5 sm:p-6" aria-labelledby="acknowledgements-title">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
              <div>
                <h2 id="acknowledgements-title" className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">Review acknowledgements</h2>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">Choose the statements that apply to this review.</p>
              </div>
              <button type="button" onClick={selectRecommended} className="secondary-action px-3 py-2 text-[11px]">Select recommended</button>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
              {items.map((item) => (
                <label key={item.id} className={`flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border p-4 transition ${item.selected ? 'border-teal-300 bg-teal-50/70' : 'border-slate-200 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50'}`}>
                  <input type="checkbox" className="sr-only" checked={item.selected} onChange={() => updateSelected(item.id)} />
                  <span className={`mt-0.5 shrink-0 ${item.selected ? 'text-teal-700' : 'text-slate-400'}`} aria-hidden="true">{item.selected ? <CheckSquare className="h-5 w-5" /> : <Square className="h-5 w-5" />}</span>
                  <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <span className="inline-flex rounded-md border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 px-2 py-1 text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-600">Reviewer acknowledgement</span>
                    <span className="mt-2 block text-sm font-semibold leading-5 text-slate-800">{item.title}</span>
                  </span>
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>

            <label htmlFor="reviewer-note" className="mt-5 block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">Optional reviewer note</label>
            <textarea id="reviewer-note" value={reviewerNote} onChange={(event) => { setReviewerNote(event.target.value); setReceipt(null); setReceiptEstimate(null); }} rows={3} maxLength={500} placeholder="Add a short note to include with each selected acknowledgement…" className="mt-2 w-full resize-y rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-teal-700" />
            <p className="mt-1 text-right text-[10px] text-slate-400">{reviewerNote.length}/500</p>
          </section>

          <aside className="space-y-4">
            <section className="surface p-5 sm:p-6">
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                  <p className="eyebrow">Receipt preview</p>
                  <h2 className="mt-1 font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">Review summary</h2>
                </div>
                <Clipboard className="h-5 w-5 text-teal-700" />
              </div>
              <dl className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <SummaryField label="Patient" value={`${patient.name} · ${patient.bed}`} />
                <SummaryField label="Model estimate" value={isLoadingPatient ? 'Loading…' : modelEstimate === null ? 'Unavailable' : `${modelEstimate.toFixed(1)}%`} />
              </dl>
              <p className="mt-3 text-[10px] leading-4 text-slate-500">Estimate is loaded from this patient’s backend model result.</p>
              <div className="mt-5 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 text-xs">
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-600">Selected acknowledgements</span>
                <span className="font-mono font-bold text-slate-900">{selectedCount}</span>
              </div>
              <button type="button" onClick={submitReceipt} disabled={isSubmitting || selectedCount === 0} className="primary-action mt-4 w-full disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-teal-900/40 disabled:text-white/80 disabled:shadow-none">
                {isSubmitting ? 'Saving simulated receipt…' : <><Check className="h-4 w-4" /> Create review receipt</>}
              </button>
              {error && <p role="alert" className="mt-3 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 p-3 text-xs text-rose-800">{error}</p>}
            </section>

            {receipt && (
              <section className="surface p-5 sm:p-6" aria-labelledby="receipt-title">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                  <div><p className="eyebrow">Saved acknowledgement</p><h2 id="receipt-title" className="mt-1 font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">Review receipt</h2></div>
                  <span className="rounded-full bg-emerald-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-800">{receipt.status === 'SIMULATED_RECEIPT_RECORDED' ? 'Simulated receipt recorded' : receipt.status}</span>
                </div>
                <dl className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <SummaryField label="Timestamp" value={receipt.timestamp} />
                  <SummaryField label="Reviewer" value={receipt.signed_by} />
                  <SummaryField label="Patient" value={`${patient.name} · ${receipt.patient_id}`} />
                  <SummaryField label="Model estimate" value={receiptEstimate === null ? 'Unavailable' : `${receiptEstimate.toFixed(1)}%`} />
                </dl>
                <div className="mt-4">
                  <h3 className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Acknowledgements</h3>
                  <ul className="mt-2 space-y-2">{receipt.orders.map((order, index) => <li key={`${index}-${order}`} className="rounded-lg bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-700">{order}</li>)}</ul>
                </div>
                <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-950 p-3 text-white">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-300">SHA-256 hash · not a digital signature</p>
                    <button type="button" onClick={copyHash} className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-slate-700 px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-800"><Copy className="h-3 w-3" />{copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy'}</button>
                  </div>
                  <code className="mt-2 block break-all font-mono text-[10px] leading-4 text-teal-200">{receipt.integrity_signature_sha256}</code>
                </div>
              </section>
            )}
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

const SummaryField = ({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) => (
  <div className="min-w-0 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2.5">
    <dt className="text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">{label}</dt>
    <dd className="mt-1 break-words text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800">{value}</dd>
  </div>
);

FILE: vigil-frontend/src/components/screens/QueueScreen.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Activity, AlertTriangle, ArrowRight, Clock, Filter, Search, ShieldAlert } from 'lucide-react';
import { ScreenId, Patient } from '../../types';
import { PATIENTS } from '../../data/mockData';
import { BackendPatientDetail, BackendPatientSummary, fetchPatientRisk, fetchPatients } from '../../services/api';
import { getRiskBand, RISK_BANDS, RISK_BAND_NOTE } from '../../constants/clinicalDisplay';

interface QueueScreenProps {
  activePatient: Patient;
  onSelectPatient: (patient: Patient) => void;
  onNavigate: (screen: ScreenId) => void;
}

interface QueueRow {
  summary: BackendPatientSummary;
  detail: BackendPatientDetail | null;
  patient: Patient;
}

const fallbackRows = (): QueueRow[] => PATIENTS.map((patient) => ({
  summary: { id: patient.bed, name: patient.name, bed: patient.bed, primary_dx: patient.primaryDiagnosis },
  detail: null,
  patient,
}));

export const QueueScreen: React.FC<QueueScreenProps> = ({ activePatient, onSelectPatient, onNavigate }) => {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<QueueRow[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [backendConnected, setBackendConnected] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isOfflineFallback, setIsOfflineFallback] = useState(false);
  const [filterRisk, setFilterRisk] = useState('all');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    setBackendConnected(false);
    setIsOfflineFallback(false);
    fetchPatients()
      .then(async (summaries) => {
        const details = await Promise.all(summaries.map(async (summary) => {
          try { return await fetchPatientRisk(summary.id, false); } catch { return null; }
        }));
        if (cancelled) return;
        setBackendConnected(true);
        const nextRows = summaries.map((summary, index) => {
          const detail = details[index];
          const template = PATIENTS.find((patient) => patient.bed === summary.bed) ?? PATIENTS[0];
          const patient: Patient = {
            ...template,
            id: summary.id,
            name: summary.name,
            bed: summary.bed,
            primaryDiagnosis: summary.primary_dx,
            ...(detail ? {
              age: detail.age,
              gender: detail.gender === 'F' ? 'F' as const : 'M' as const,
              sepsisRisk: detail.sepsis_risk_percentage,
              vitals: template.vitals.map((vital, vitalIndex) => vitalIndex === template.vitals.length - 1 ? {
                ...vital,
                hr: detail.vitals.HR ?? vital.hr,
                map: detail.vitals.MAP ?? vital.map,
                rr: detail.vitals.Resp ?? vital.rr,
                spo2: detail.vitals.O2Sat ?? vital.spo2,
              } : vital),
            } : {}),
          };
          return { summary, detail, patient };
        });
        setRows(nextRows);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setRows(fallbackRows());
        setIsOfflineFallback(true);
        setBackendConnected(false);
      })
      .finally(() => { if (!cancelled) setIsLoading(false); });
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, []);

  const isLive = backendConnected && !isOfflineFallback;
  const withRisk = rows.filter((row) => row.detail !== null);
  const highCount = withRisk.filter((row) => getRiskBand(row.detail?.sepsis_risk_percentage ?? 0) === 'high').length;
  const elevatedCount = withRisk.filter((row) => getRiskBand(row.detail?.sepsis_risk_percentage ?? 0) === 'elevated').length;
  const filteredRows = useMemo(() => rows.filter(({ summary, detail, patient }) => {
    const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    if (!`${summary.name} ${summary.bed} ${summary.primary_dx}`.toLowerCase().includes(query)) return false;
    if (!detail && !isOfflineFallback && filterRisk !== 'all') return false;
    const risk = detail?.sepsis_risk_percentage ?? (isOfflineFallback ? patient.sepsisRisk : null);
    if (filterRisk !== 'all' && risk === null) return false;
    if (filterRisk !== 'all') return getRiskBand(risk) === filterRisk;
    return true;
  }), [rows, searchQuery, filterRisk, isOfflineFallback]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen pb-16">
      <div className="page-shell space-y-5 pt-6 sm:pt-8">
        <header className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-b border-slate-200/80 pb-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-end lg:justify-between">
          <div>
            <div className="eyebrow">Seeded demo cohort</div>
            <h1 className="page-title mt-2">Patient queue</h1>
            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">Review the seeded cohort and open a patient’s model explanation.</p>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 sm:gap-3">
            <Metric icon={<ShieldAlert />} label="High estimate" value={isLive ? `${highCount} ${highCount === 1 ? 'patient' : 'patients'}` : '—'} tone="rose" />
            <Metric icon={<AlertTriangle />} label="Elevated" value={isLive ? `${elevatedCount} ${elevatedCount === 1 ? 'patient' : 'patients'}` : '—'} tone="amber" />
            <Metric icon={<Clock />} label="Results loaded" value={isLive ? `${withRisk.length} / ${rows.length}` : '—'} tone="teal" />
          </div>
        </header>

        {isOfflineFallback && <div role="status" className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-4 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-900">Backend offline · showing sample profiles. Sample scores are not live model results.</div>}
        {error && <div role="alert" className="rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-rose-800">{error}</div>}

        <section className="surface p-3 sm:p-4" aria-label="Search and filter patients">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 xl:flex-row xl:items-center xl:justify-between">
            <label className="relative block w-full xl:max-w-sm">
              <Search className="absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
              <input value={searchQuery} onChange={(event) => setSearchQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search name, bed, or diagnosis" className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 py-3 pl-10 pr-3 text-xs font-medium text-slate-800 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500" />
            </label>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1 xl:pb-0">
              <span className="mr-1 inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400"><Filter className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Risk filter</span>
              {([['all', 'All'], ['low', `Low <${RISK_BANDS.low.max}%`], ['elevated', `Elevated ${RISK_BANDS.elevated.min}–<${RISK_BANDS.elevated.max}%`], ['high', `High ≥${RISK_BANDS.high.min}%`]] as const).map(([id, label]) => {
                const band = id === 'all' ? null : RISK_BANDS[id];
                return <button key={id} onClick={() => setFilterRisk(id)} className={`shrink-0 rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-[11px] font-semibold ${filterRisk === id ? band ? `${band.background} ${band.text} ${band.border}` : 'border-slate-700 bg-[#10263a] text-white' : 'border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50'}`}>{label}</button>;
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between"><h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">Demo cohort</h2><span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-500">{filteredRows.length} {filteredRows.length === 1 ? 'patient' : 'patients'} <span className="hidden sm:inline">· {RISK_BAND_NOTE}</span></span></div>
        {isLoading ? <div className="surface p-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">Loading patient records…</div> : filteredRows.length === 0 ? <div className="surface p-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-600">No patients match this search or filter.</div> : (
          <div className="grid gap-3">
            {filteredRows.map(({ summary, detail, patient }) => {
              const risk = detail?.sepsis_risk_percentage ?? patient.sepsisRisk;
              const riskTone = getRiskBand(risk);
              const riskText = detail ? `${risk.toFixed(1)}%` : isOfflineFallback ? `${risk}% sample` : 'Unavailable';
              return (
                <button key={summary.id} type="button" onClick={() => { onSelectPatient(patient); onNavigate('risk'); }} className={`group relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border bg-white p-4 text-left shadow-sm transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-lg sm:p-5 ${activePatient.bed === summary.bed ? 'border-teal-300 ring-2 ring-teal-100' : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300'}`}>
                  <span className={`absolute inset-y-0 left-0 w-1 ${RISK_BANDS[riskTone].solid}`} />
                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 pl-2 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                    <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-4">
                      <span className="flex h-12 w-14 shrink-0 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-50 text-center"><span className="text-[8px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">Bed</span><span className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-700">{summary.bed}</span></span>
                      <span className="min-w-0"><span className="font-display text-base font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900">{summary.name}</span><span className="mt-0.5 block truncate text-xs text-slate-500">{summary.primary_dx}</span><span className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-500"><span><Activity className="mr-1 inline h-3 w-3" />HR {detail?.vitals.HR ?? '—'}</span><span>MAP {detail?.vitals.MAP ?? '—'}</span><span>SpO₂ {detail?.vitals.O2Sat ?? '—'}%</span></span></span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 sm:justify-end">
                      <span className={`rounded-xl border px-3 py-2 text-right ${RISK_BANDS[riskTone].background} ${RISK_BANDS[riskTone].text} ${RISK_BANDS[riskTone].border}`}><span className="block text-[8px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] opacity-70">{RISK_BANDS[riskTone].label} estimate</span><span className="mt-0.5 block font-mono text-lg font-bold leading-none">{riskText}</span></span>
                      <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-slate-200 text-slate-400 transition group-hover:border-teal-300 group-hover:bg-[#e9f5f2] group-hover:text-teal-700"><ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" /></span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

const Metric = ({ icon, label, value, tone }: { icon: React.ReactNode; label: string; value: string; tone: 'rose' | 'amber' | 'teal' }) => <div className="min-w-0 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-2.5 py-2 sm:px-3.5"><div className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[8px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] sm:text-[9px] ${tone === 'rose' ? 'text-rose-700' : tone === 'amber' ? 'text-amber-700' : 'text-teal-700'}`}>{React.cloneElement(icon as React.ReactElement<{ className?: string }>, { className: 'h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0' })}<span className="truncate">{label}</span></div><div className="mt-1 font-mono text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 sm:text-sm">{value}</div></div>;

================================================================================
FILE: vigil-frontend/src/components/screens/RiskAttributionScreen.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Activity, ArrowRight, Cpu, SlidersHorizontal, Sparkles, WandSparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { ScreenId, Patient } from '../../types';
import { BackendPatientDetail, FeatureContribution, fetchModelInfo, fetchPatientRisk, ModelInfo } from '../../services/api';
import { ModelInfoPanel } from '../ModelInfoPanel';
import { displayFeatureName, displayFeatureText, FEATURE_UNITS, getRiskBand, RISK_BANDS, RISK_BAND_NOTE } from '../../constants/clinicalDisplay';

interface RiskAttributionScreenProps { patient: Patient; onNavigate: (screen: ScreenId) => void; }

export const RiskAttributionScreen: React.FC<RiskAttributionScreenProps> = ({ patient, onNavigate }) => {
  const [backendData, setBackendData] = useState<BackendPatientDetail | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedFeature, setSelectedFeature] = useState<FeatureContribution | null>(null);
  const [modelInfo, setModelInfo] = useState<ModelInfo | null>(null);
  const [narrationLoading, setNarrationLoading] = useState(false);
  const [narrationError, setNarrationError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    setBackendData(null);
    fetchPatientRisk(patient.bed, false)
      .then((data) => { if (!cancelled) setBackendData(data); })
      .catch((reason: unknown) => { if (!cancelled) setError(reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : 'Unable to load model results.'); })
      .finally(() => { if (!cancelled) setIsLoading(false); });
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [patient.bed]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetchModelInfo().then((info) => { if (!cancelled) setModelInfo(info); }).catch(() => undefined);
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, []);

  const contributions = useMemo(() => [...(backendData?.shap_contributions ?? [])].sort((a, b) => Math.abs(b.shap_impact) - Math.abs(a.shap_impact)), [backendData]);
  useEffect(() => { setSelectedFeature(contributions[0] ?? null); }, [contributions]);
  const maxImpact = Math.max(...contributions.map((item) => Math.abs(item.shap_impact)), 0.001);
  const riskBand = backendData ? getRiskBand(backendData.sepsis_risk_percentage) : null;
  const riskDisplay = riskBand ? RISK_BANDS[riskBand] : null;
  const riskColor = riskBand === 'high' ? 'text-rose-300' : riskBand === 'elevated' ? 'text-amber-300' : 'text-emerald-300';
  const riskBar = riskBand === 'high' ? 'bg-rose-400' : riskBand === 'elevated' ? 'bg-amber-300' : 'bg-emerald-300';

  const generateNarration = async () => {
    if (narrationLoading) return;
    setNarrationLoading(true);
    setNarrationError(null);
    try {
      const narrated = await fetchPatientRisk(patient.bed, true);
      setBackendData(narrated);
    } catch (reason) {
      setNarrationError(reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : 'Could not generate a summary.');
    } finally {
      setNarrationLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const narrationIsTemplate = backendData?.narration_source
    ? backendData.narration_source === 'template'
    : !backendData?.narration_model || backendData.narration_model === 'Template summary (LLM unavailable)';
  const narrationSourceLabel = backendData?.narration_source === 'live'
    ? 'Live'
    : backendData?.narration_source === 'cached'
      ? `Saved AI summary${backendData.narration_generated_at ? `, generated ${new Date(backendData.narration_generated_at).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })}` : ''}`
      : 'Template summary (LLM unavailable)';

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen pb-16">
      <div className="page-shell space-y-4 pt-4 sm:space-y-5 sm:pt-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-slate-200/80 pb-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="eyebrow flex items-center gap-2"><Cpu className="h-4 w-4" /> Model interpretation</p>
            <h2 className="page-title mt-1.5">Risk attribution</h2>
            <p className="mt-1.5 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">Review the estimate alongside its inputs and leading contributors. This is decision support, not a diagnosis.</p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex w-fit items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 shadow-sm">
            <span className="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-[#e9f5f2] text-[10px] font-bold text-[#075c57]">{patient.name.split(' ').map((part) => part[0]).slice(0, 2).join('')}</span>
            <span><span className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">{patient.name}</span><span className="block font-mono text-[10px] text-slate-500">{patient.bed}</span></span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {isLoading && <div className="surface p-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">Loading patient estimate and feature attribution…</div>}
        {error && <div role="alert" className="rounded-2xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 p-4 text-sm text-rose-900">Could not load this patient’s model result: {error}. No sample estimate is shown.</div>}

        {backendData && <>
          <div className="grid items-stretch gap-4 xl:grid-cols-2">
            <section className="relative flex h-full min-h-[320px] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-[24px] bg-[#10263a] p-5 text-white shadow-lg shadow-[#10263a]/10 sm:p-6">
              <div className="pointer-events-none absolute -right-16 -top-20 h-56 w-56 rounded-full bg-teal-400/10 blur-3xl" />
              <div className="relative flex flex-1 flex-col">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-slate-300">Model estimate</p>
                    <div className={`mt-3 font-display text-6xl font-semibold leading-none tracking-[-0.06em] ${riskColor}`}>{backendData.sepsis_risk_percentage.toFixed(1)}<span className="ml-1 text-3xl">%</span></div>
                  </div>
                  <span className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.08] p-3 text-teal-200"><Activity className="h-6 w-6" /></span>
                </div>
                <div className="mt-5 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/10"><div className={`h-full rounded-full ${riskBar}`} style={{ width: `${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, backendData.sepsis_risk_percentage))}%` }} /></div>
                <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <span className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold ${riskDisplay?.background} ${riskDisplay?.text}`}>{riskDisplay?.label}</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-300">Baseline {modelInfo ? `${(modelInfo.positive_rate * 100).toFixed(1)}%` : '—'}</span>
                </div>
                <p className="mt-2 text-[9px] leading-4 text-slate-400">{RISK_BAND_NOTE}</p>
                <p className="mt-auto max-w-xl pt-5 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-300">A research model output for review alongside the patient’s full clinical context. It is not a diagnosis or a care recommendation.</p>
              </div>
            </section>

            <section className="surface flex h-full min-h-[320px] flex-col p-5 sm:p-6">
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#e9f5f2] text-[#0b766e]"><Sparkles className="h-5 w-5" /></span>
                  <div><p className="eyebrow">Narrative summary</p><h3 className="mt-1 font-display text-lg font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900">What is driving this estimate?</h3></div>
                </div>
                {backendData.ai_narration && <span className={`max-w-[55%] rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-right text-[9px] font-bold tracking-wide ${narrationIsTemplate ? 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600' : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800'}`}>{narrationSourceLabel}</span>}
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 py-4">
                {backendData.ai_narration ? <p className="rounded-xl border border-slate-100 border-l-[3px] border-l-teal-600 bg-slate-50/80 px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-700">{displayFeatureText(backendData.ai_narration)}</p> : <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/80 p-4"><p className="max-w-3xl text-xs leading-5 text-slate-600">Generate an optional narrative summary for this estimate. This makes a Groq API request.</p><button onClick={generateNarration} disabled={narrationLoading} className="primary-action mt-3 min-h-10 px-3 py-2 text-xs disabled:cursor-wait disabled:opacity-60"><WandSparkles className="h-4 w-4" />{narrationLoading ? 'Generating summary…' : 'Generate summary'}</button></div>}
                {backendData.ai_narration && backendData.narration_source === 'live' && backendData.narration_model && <p className="mt-2 text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-500">Groq · {backendData.narration_model}</p>}
                {backendData.ai_narration && backendData.narration_source && <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-[10px] text-slate-500">
                  {backendData.narration_limit_reached
                    ? <span className="font-semibold text-amber-800">{backendData.narration_source === 'cached' ? 'Demo limit reached - showing the saved AI summary' : 'Demo limit reached - showing the template summary'}</span>
                    : <span>Live AI summaries remaining today: {backendData.live_narrations_remaining} of {backendData.live_narrations_limit}</span>}
                </div>}
                {narrationError && <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-xs text-rose-700">Summary request failed: {narrationError}</p>}
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 border-t border-slate-100 pt-3 sm:flex-row">
                <button onClick={() => onNavigate('counterfactual')} className="primary-action min-h-10 px-3 py-2 text-xs sm:w-auto"><SlidersHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" /> Explore a model scenario <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /></button>
              </div>
            </section>
          </div>

          <section className="surface p-5 sm:p-7">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="eyebrow flex items-center gap-2"><Activity className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Model input snapshot</p>
                <h3 className="mt-1 font-display text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900">Patient measurements</h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">Fixed values used by the model. Header vitals are a simulated stream.</p>
              </div>
              <span className="w-fit rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-600">{Object.keys(backendData.vitals).length} features</span>
            </div>
            {(() => {
              const vitalNames = new Set(['HR', 'MAP', 'Resp', 'Temp', 'O2Sat']);
              const rows = Object.entries(backendData.vitals);
              const vitalRows = rows.filter(([feature]) => vitalNames.has(feature));
              const labRows = rows.filter(([feature]) => !vitalNames.has(feature));
              const renderRows = (items: typeof rows) => (
                <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto_auto] gap-3 bg-slate-50 px-4 py-2.5 text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-slate-500">
                    <span>Measurement</span><span className="text-right">Result</span><span className="w-16 text-left">Unit</span>
                  </div>
                  {items.map(([feature, value], index) => (
                    <div key={feature} className={`grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto_auto] items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 ${index ? 'border-t border-slate-100' : ''}`}>
                      <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">{displayFeatureName(feature)}</span>
                      <span className="min-w-12 text-right font-mono text-sm font-semibold tabular-nums text-slate-900">{value}</span>
                      <span className="w-16 text-left text-[10px] text-slate-500">{FEATURE_UNITS[feature] || '—'}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                  {items.length === 0 && <p className="px-4 py-5 text-xs text-slate-500">No measurements available.</p>}
                </div>
              );
              return (
                <div className="grid gap-5 pt-5 md:grid-cols-2">
                  <div><h4 className="mb-2 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-slate-600">Vital signs</h4>{renderRows(vitalRows)}</div>
                  <div><h4 className="mb-2 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-slate-600">Laboratory results</h4>{renderRows(labRows)}</div>
                </div>
              );
            })()}
          </section>

          <section className="surface p-5 sm:p-7">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
              <div><p className="eyebrow flex items-center gap-2"><Activity className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Feature attribution</p><h3 className="mt-1 font-display text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900">Factors behind the estimate</h3><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">Sorted by absolute SHAP impact. SHAP contributions are in log-odds units, not percentage points.</p></div>
              <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-600">{contributions.length} model features</span>
            </div>
            {contributions.length === 0 ? <p className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">No feature contributions were returned.</p> : (
              <div className="grid gap-5 pt-5 lg:grid-cols-[1.2fr_0.8fr]">
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {contributions.map((item) => {
                    const positive = item.direction === 'increases_risk';
                    const selected = selectedFeature?.feature === item.feature;
                    return (
                      <button key={item.feature} type="button" onClick={() => setSelectedFeature(item)} className={`w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-left transition ${selected ? 'border-teal-300 bg-[#eff8f5]' : 'border-slate-100 bg-white hover:border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50/80'}`}>
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                          <div className="min-w-0"><span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">{displayFeatureName(item.feature)}</span><span className="ml-2 text-[10px] text-slate-500">Observed {item.value} {FEATURE_UNITS[item.feature]}</span></div>
                          <span className={`shrink-0 font-mono text-xs font-bold ${positive ? 'text-rose-700' : 'text-emerald-700'}`}>{item.shap_impact > 0 ? '+' : ''}{item.shap_impact.toFixed(4)}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-2.5 h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-100"><div className={`h-full rounded-full ${positive ? 'bg-rose-500' : 'bg-emerald-500'}`} style={{ width: `${Math.max(2, Math.abs(item.shap_impact) / maxImpact * 100)}%` }} /></div>
                        <div className={`mt-1.5 text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] ${positive ? 'text-rose-700' : 'text-emerald-700'}`}>{positive ? 'Increases risk estimate' : 'Decreases risk estimate'}</div>
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
                <aside className="rounded-2xl bg-[#f4f7f8] p-5 lg:sticky lg:top-56 lg:self-start">
                  {selectedFeature ? <>
                    <p className="eyebrow">Selected factor</p>
                    <h4 className="mt-2 font-display text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900">{displayFeatureName(selectedFeature.feature)}</h4>
                    <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                      <div className="rounded-xl border border-white bg-white p-3"><p className="text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Observed value</p><p className="mt-1 font-mono text-lg font-bold text-slate-800">{selectedFeature.value} {FEATURE_UNITS[selectedFeature.feature]}</p></div>
                      <div className="rounded-xl border border-white bg-white p-3"><p className="text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">SHAP impact</p><p className={`mt-1 font-mono text-lg font-bold ${selectedFeature.direction === 'increases_risk' ? 'text-rose-700' : 'text-emerald-700'}`}>{selectedFeature.shap_impact > 0 ? '+' : ''}{selectedFeature.shap_impact.toFixed(4)}</p></div>
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-4 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-600">This attribution describes how the model used this feature for the current estimate. It does not establish causality or predict the effect of treatment.</p>
                  </> : <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Select a factor to inspect its value and contribution.</p>}
                </aside>
              </div>
            )}
          </section>
        </>}

        <ModelInfoPanel compact />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export type RiskBand = 'low' | 'elevated' | 'high';

export const RISK_BANDS = {
  low: { min: 0, max: 15, label: 'Low', text: 'text-emerald-800', background: 'bg-emerald-50', border: 'border-emerald-200', solid: 'bg-emerald-500' },
  elevated: { min: 15, max: 40, label: 'Elevated', text: 'text-amber-800', background: 'bg-amber-50', border: 'border-amber-200', solid: 'bg-amber-500' },
  high: { min: 40, max: 100, label: 'High', text: 'text-rose-800', background: 'bg-rose-50', border: 'border-rose-200', solid: 'bg-rose-500' },
} as const;

export const RISK_BAND_NOTE = 'Illustrative display bands, not clinical thresholds';

export function getRiskBand(risk: number): RiskBand {
  if (risk < RISK_BANDS.low.max) return 'low';
  if (risk < RISK_BANDS.elevated.max) return 'elevated';
  return 'high';
}

export const FEATURE_DISPLAY_NAMES: Record<string, string> = {
  HR: 'Heart rate',
  MAP: 'MAP',
  Resp: 'Respiratory rate',
  Temp: 'Temperature',
  O2Sat: 'SpO₂',
  Lactate: 'Lactate',
  WBC: 'WBC',
  Platelets: 'Platelets',
  Creatinine: 'Creatinine',
  HCO3: 'Bicarbonate (HCO₃)',
  BUN: 'BUN',
  Glucose: 'Glucose',
  Bilirubin_total: 'Bilirubin (total)',
};

export const FEATURE_UNITS: Record<string, string> = {
  HR: 'bpm', MAP: 'mmHg', Resp: '/min', Temp: '°C', O2Sat: '%', Lactate: 'mmol/L',
  WBC: '×10⁹/L', Platelets: '×10⁹/L', Creatinine: 'mg/dL', HCO3: 'mmol/L',
  BUN: 'mg/dL', Glucose: 'mg/dL', Bilirubin_total: 'mg/dL',
};

export function displayFeatureName(feature: string): string {
  return FEATURE_DISPLAY_NAMES[feature] ?? feature;
}

export function displayFeatureText(text: string): string {
  return Object.entries(FEATURE_DISPLAY_NAMES).reduce(
    (result, [key, label]) => result.replace(new RegExp(`\\b${key}\\b`, 'g'), label),
    text,
  );
}

FILE: vigil-frontend/src/hooks/useTelemetry.ts
================================================================================

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { API_BASE_URL } from '../services/api';

export interface TelemetryData {
  patient_id: string;
  hr: number;
  map: number;
  o2sat: number;
  resp: number;
  timestamp: string;
}

export type TelemetryStatus = 'live' | 'connecting' | 'offline';

export function useTelemetry(patientId: string | null) {
  const [telemetry, setTelemetry] = useState<TelemetryData | null>(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<TelemetryStatus>('offline');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!patientId) {
      setTelemetry(null);
      setStatus('offline');
      return;
    }

    const wsBase = API_BASE_URL.replace(/^http/, 'ws').replace(/\/api$/, '');
    const wsUrl = `${wsBase}/ws/telemetry/${encodeURIComponent(patientId)}`;
    let socket: WebSocket | null = null;
    let reconnectTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    let cancelled = false;

    const connect = () => {
      if (cancelled) return;
      setStatus('connecting');
      socket = new WebSocket(wsUrl);
      socket.onopen = () => { if (!cancelled) setStatus('live'); };
      socket.onmessage = (event) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        try {
          const packet = JSON.parse(event.data) as TelemetryData | { error: string };
          if ('error' in packet) {
            setStatus('offline');
            socket?.close();
          } else {
            setTelemetry(packet);
          }
        } catch {
          setStatus('offline');
        }
      };
      socket.onerror = () => socket?.close();
      socket.onclose = () => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setStatus('offline');
        reconnectTimer = setTimeout(connect, 3000);
      };
    };

    connect();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      if (reconnectTimer) clearTimeout(reconnectTimer);
      socket?.close();
    };
  }, [patientId]);

  return { telemetry, status };
}

FILE: vigil-frontend/src/services/api.ts
================================================================================

const DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL = 'http://localhost:8000/api';

export const API_BASE_URL = (import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\/$/, '');

export interface BackendPatientSummary {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  bed: string;
  primary_dx: string;
}

export interface FeatureContribution {
  feature: string;
  value: number;
  shap_impact: number;
  direction: 'increases_risk' | 'decreases_risk';
}

export interface BackendPatientDetail extends BackendPatientSummary {
  age: number;
  gender: string;
  vitals: Record<string, number>;
  sepsis_risk_percentage: number;
  shap_contributions: FeatureContribution[];
  ai_narration: string | null;
  narration_model: string | null;
  narration_source: 'live' | 'cached' | 'template' | null;
  narration_generated_at: string | null;
  live_narrations_remaining: number;
  live_narrations_limit: number;
  narration_limit_reached: boolean;
}

export interface SimulationResult {
  patient_id: string;
  baseline_risk_percentage: number;
  simulated_risk_percentage: number;
  risk_change_percentage_points: number;
  overrides_applied: Record<string, number>;
  simulated_contributions: FeatureContribution[];
}

export interface SignOrderPayload {
  patient_id: string;
  clinician_name: string;
  selected_orders: string[];
}

export interface OrderSignResponse {
  status: string;
  note: string;
  patient_id: string;
  signed_by: string;
  order_count: number;
  orders: string[];
  timestamp: string;
  integrity_signature_sha256: string;
}

export interface ModelInfo {
  dataset: string;
  n_patients_used: number;
  n_train: number;
  n_test: number;
  positives?: number;
  test_positive_count?: number;
  test_positive_rate?: number;
  auroc_ci95?: [number, number];
  positive_rate: number;
  AUROC: number;
  auroc: number;
  average_precision: number;
  brier_score: number;
  confusion_counts: { tn: number; fp: number; fn: number; tp: number };
  date: string;
  feature_list: string[];
  calibration: string;
}

export interface HealthStatus {
  status: string;
  service: string;
  model_loaded: boolean;
  model_path_exists: boolean;
  model_features: string[];
  calibrator_loaded: boolean;
  groq_api_key_configured: boolean;
  narration_model: string;
  demo_patient_count: number;
}

async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${path}`, {
    ...init,
    signal: init?.signal ?? AbortSignal.timeout(7000),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = `HTTP ${response.status}`;
    try {
      const body = await response.json() as { detail?: string };
      if (body.detail) detail = body.detail;
    } catch {
      // Keep the HTTP status when the server does not return JSON.
    }
    throw new Error(detail);
  }
  return response.json() as Promise<T>;
}

export function fetchPatients(): Promise<BackendPatientSummary[]> {
  return request('/patients');
}

export function fetchPatientRisk(id: string, narrate = true): Promise<BackendPatientDetail> {
  return request(`/patients/${encodeURIComponent(id)}?narrate=${narrate}`);
}

export function simulateCounterfactual(
  id: string,
  overrides: Record<string, number>,
): Promise<SimulationResult> {
  return request('/simulate', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ patient_id: id, overrides }),
  });
}

export function signOrderBundle(payload: SignOrderPayload): Promise<OrderSignResponse> {
  return request('/orders/sign-and-transmit', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
  });
}

export function fetchModelInfo(): Promise<ModelInfo> {
  return request('/model-info');
}

export function fetchHealth(): Promise<HealthStatus> {
  const origin = API_BASE_URL.replace(/\/api\/?$/, '');
  const response = fetch(`${origin}/health`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(7000) });
  return response.then((result) => {
    if (!result.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${result.status}`);
    return result.json() as Promise<HealthStatus>;
  });
}

/// <reference types="vite/client" />
